# Acquisition guide replacements

Replaces all 725 stored Mount Journal, Pet Journal and Toy Box handoffs (682 still reached the UI before this change; 43 already had researched overrides).

The compiled Lua file contains only static text keyed by spell, species or item ID. Research HTML and full external databases are never loaded by the addon. Existing character quest progression and map navigation continue through the bounded source index.

Retired events explain their original requirements and availability. Non-collectible opponents and unreleased entries are identified explicitly; no fabricated farming route is supplied. Gleam remains unconfirmed.

Validation: all 725 identities still exist, their real acquisition getter returns the recorded guide, all guides have evidence references, and no loaded catalog script contains the journal handoff wording.

## Phoenix Wishwing (pets 3292, patch 10.0)

Complete Tale of the Phoenix from Tarjin the Blind in The Waking Shores (16.1, 62.6) to receive Phoenix Wishwing.

Objectives: - Ash Feather x 20 - Smoldering Phoenix Ash x 15 - Phoenix Ash Talisman

Below is a detailed explanation on how to obtain the Phoenix Wishwing pet.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Secrets.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=193373)

## Igneoid (pets 3295, patch 10.0)

Find a wild Igneoid in The Waking Shores (51.4, 31.6), start a pet battle, reduce its health below 35%, and use your trap. Win the battle to keep the captured pet.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Zones.lua)

## Winding Slitherdrake (mounts 368893, patch 10.1)

Follow the Embers of Neltharion campaign into Zaralek Cavern and complete chapter 4, Inherited Sin. Ebyssian's final quest, Worst of the Worst, at Slitherdrake Roost (53.6, 30.2), rewards the Winding Slitherdrake after you defeat Overseer Xymra. Continue the campaign prerequisites shown in the quest-chain notes if this quest is not available yet.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=74563), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=204361)

## Subterranean Magmammoth (mounts 371176, patch 10.1)

Buy Subterranean Magmammoth from Saccratros in Zaralek Cavern (55.9, 55.4) for 100 Coveted Bauble.

## Seething Slug (mounts 374138, patch 10.1)

Loot Seething Cache in Zaralek Cavern (32.3, 39.4) to obtain Seething Slug.

You'll need to pick up 3x stacks of a Insidious Insight debuff from Seething Orbs located in the Zaqali Caldera . Warning: While you may click each Seething Orb with multiple players, when the first player clicks the Seething Cache, the debuff will be removed from all nearby players and the cache itself will be gone!

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Zones.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=192779)

## Tarecgosa's Visage (mounts 407555, patch 10.1)

You must own Dragonwrath, Tarecgosa's Rest and complete Kalecgos's blue dragonflight story in Dragonflight, ending with A New Dawn. With the legendary staff available, speak to Kalecgos at the Veiled Ossuary in Thaldraszus (65.7, 12.6) for Rhapsody in Blue. Prepare Tarecgosa's grave and complete the ritual to learn her visage as a permanent mount. This does not consume the staff.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=73199), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=407555)

## Big Slick in the City (mounts 408313, patch 10.1)

Complete Snailed It from Briggul in Zaralek Cavern (44.2, 80.0) to receive Big Slick in the City.

Introduce yourself to Slick.

Requires Renown 7 with Loamm Niffen.

Reach rank 5 with Glimerogg Racer by completing daily snail training quests for Briggul in Glimmerogg.

## Cataloged Shalewing (mounts 408651, patch 10.1)

Join Researchers Under Fire at the central platform in Zaralek Cavern (47.5, 56.7), when the event is active. Help the researchers finish the objectives and open your weekly researcher reward bag. Cataloged Shalewing is a chance reward from the researcher bags, so finishing the event does not guarantee the mount. Complete as many objectives as possible for the better reward tier.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=205204), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=74906)

## Boulder Hauler (mounts 408653, patch 10.1)

Reach Renown 3 with the Loamm Niffen to unlock bartering, then earn Barter Bricks through Zaralek Cavern activities and Ponzo's barter quests. Buy the Boulder Hauler from Ponzo in Loamm (58.1, 53.8). His offers include 170 Barter Bricks or 85 Barter Boulders; the boulder currency becomes available as your Niffen bartering unlocks advance.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=205209), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/npc=204693)

## Blaise (pets 3511, patch 10.1)

Take part in Fyrakk Assaults when active in Ohn'ahran Plains or the Azure Span. Loot the assault enemies and the event reward chest for a chance at Blaise. To open a Secured Shipment, gather the assault wards and exchange three Everburning Keys at the shipment. Complete the weekly assault as well; the pet is a random event drop rather than a wild pet you can trap.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=205002), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/object=398815)

## Ambre (pets 3512, patch 10.1)

Take part in the active Fyrakk Assault in Ohn'ahran Plains or the Azure Span. Ambre can be looted from assault enemies and the Secured Shipment. Collect assault wards to obtain Everburning Keys, then use three keys to open the shipment. Repeat eligible assault activities for another chance; Ambre cannot be captured in a wild pet battle.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=205003), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/object=398815)

## Valiance (mounts 413922, patch 10.1.5)

Unlock old Scholomance and the Wards of the Dread Citadel route into the restored Naxxramas content. Obtain a Rotten Delicious apple from Zackett Skullsmash in Naxxramas. Enter 10-player Naxxramas and leave Instructor Razuvious alive. Approach Valiance slowly and feed him the apple. Use an Obedience Crystal to control a Death Knight Understudy, bring the Understudy to Valiance so it mounts him, then ride back to Razuvious. Release the Understudy and kill Razuvious to loot Valiance's reins. The mount comes from this interaction, not from killing Valiance.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=206585), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/npc=206712)

## Pattie (mounts 418078, patch 10.1.5)

Complete all 15 Secrets of Azeroth clues for Whodunnit?. Start with Preservationist Kathos in the Roasted Ram inn in Valdrakken (48.4, 47.8), beginning Preserving Rarities. Follow Kathos's and Tithris's clue quests in order: recover the thinking cap, Torch of Pyrreth and Idol of Ohn'ahra, then use those tools to solve the remaining clues and expose the thief. The chain remains available after the original event. Completing Under Suspicion finishes the achievement and awards Pattie.

Evidence: [source 1](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/secrets-of-azeroth-world-event), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/achievement=18646)

## Plagued Grain (toys 206565, patch 10.1.5)

Unlock the restored Scholomance and Wards of the Dread Citadel route into old Naxxramas. Inside Naxxramas, repair the Makeshift Grappling Hook and use it to reach Zackett Skullsmash above Thaddius's room. Bring Black Coffee to wake him and complete his introduction. Exchange Scourgestones for Death's Bargaining Chips, then buy Plagued Grain from Zackett for 15 chips. The Eastern Plaguelands map marker is the Naxxramas entrance, not an outdoor location for the vendor. Zackett is a merchant; do not attack him for this toy.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=206565/plagued-grain)

## Familiar Journal (toys 208096, patch 10.1.5)

Loot Familiar Journal in Western Plaguelands (69.1, 73.0) to obtain Familiar Journal.

With 10.1.5, Blizzard readded the original version of Scholomance! Thank you, Blizzard! -Crieve Here is how to get started: 1. Obtain 'Krastinov's Bag of Horrors' from the rare spawn Doctor Theolen Krastinov in Scholomance, Heroic difficulty. This step can be skipped if you are accompanied by someone who already has the toy. 2. Defeat Darkmaster Gandling in Headmaster's Retreat and enter the upper level centre room. 3. Find a pile of bones on the ground in the southeastern part of the room, and use the toy 'Krastinov's Bag of Horrors'. 4. Eva Sarkhoff should now have spawned, but you cannot interact with her before you remove the toy visage/buff named 'Surgical Alterations'. 5. Accept Eva Sarkhoof's quest and her Inert Spectral Essence. Loot Eva's Femur from the pile of bones. 6. Walk back upstairs to The Viewing Room. There are two bookcases in the southwestern corner of the room. Eva's Journal can be found on a middle shelf on the backside of the left bookcase. 7. Obtain the reagents 3x Dark Runes and 5x Essence of Undeath and use the Inert Spectral Essence. Equip the crafted trinket 'Spectral Essence'. 8. Obtain candles from doing objectives around Caer Darrow (outside Scholomance): 8.1 Loot 'The Deed to Andorhal' from inside Andorhal Townhall at 43.35, 69.3, and give it to Magistrate Marduke at 70.5, 74.0. 8.2 Loot 'Bucket of Fountain Water' from the candlelit fountain at 68.9, 78.8, and give it to Joseph Dirte at 68.0, 74.8. 8.3 Loot 'Trampled Doll' from the meatwagon in Darrowshire at 35.7, 83.5. (Eastern Plaguelands!), return to Caer Darrow and give it to Sammy at 69.15, 78.7. 8.4 Loot 'The Road Ahead' from a wall inside old Corin's Crossing tavern at 55.0, 64.0. (Eastern Plaguelands!), return to Caer Darrow and give it to Artist Renfray at 65.8, 75.4. 8.5 Loot 'Undelivered Shipment of Smokes' from a wagon behind the fountain at King's Square in Stratholme, return to Caer Darrow and give it to Rory at 63.4, 75.5. 9. Use Eva's Journal to begin the ritual at 69.7, 71.7, inside Caer Darrow keep/open world Scholomance.

The Familiar Journal itself can be found in the Viewing Room of Old Scholomance, on the second bookshelf from the left wall, near the mini-boss Marduk Blackpool. All you have to do is pick up the book, and the toy is yours! It's as simple as that.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Instances.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=208096)

## Renewed Magmammoth (mounts 374278, patch 10.1.7)

Wait for an active Dreamsurge in one of the four original Dragon Isles zones. Join its Waking Dream portals and defeat the empowered flame lieutenants for Charred Elemental Remains. Collect 20 remains on one character, then combine them to create the Renewed Magmammoth. This is a combination reward, rather than a vendor purchase or a single guaranteed boss drop.

## Mimiron's Jumpjets (mounts 424082, patch 10.1.7)

Collect three booster parts on the same character. First, bring three players with the Torch of Pyrreth to the braziers on Jaguero Isle, Cape of Stranglethorn (59.4, 79.0), summon Enigma Ward and loot its part. Second, loot the part in Irontree Woods, Felwood (49.9, 26.4). Third, go to the Dark Portal in the Blasted Lands (54.8, 52.1), destroy the nearby Legion Flak Cannons and channel on the part without being interrupted; use Zidormi's older phase if needed. Bring all three parts to the Empowered Arcane Forge in Valdrakken (36.5, 61.9) and combine them into Mimiron's Jumpjets. Obtain the Torch through the Secrets of Azeroth clues at the Roasted Ram inn if you do not have it.

Evidence: [source 1](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/secrets-of-azeroth-world-event), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=210022)

## Winter Night Dreamsaber (mounts 424476, patch 10.2)

Plant a Small Dreamseed at an Emerald Bounty soil patch in the Emerald Dream. Contribute Emerald Dewdrops while the seed grows, wait for the bloom to finish, then loot its Dreamseed Cache. Winter Night Dreamsaber is a rare reward from these caches. Gather more Dreamseeds and Dewdrops from Dream activities and repeat at other bounty patches; an individual planting does not guarantee a mount.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=210059), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=210218)

## Golden Regal Scarab (mounts 428060, patch 10.2)

Visit Madam Goya's Black Market Auction House in Valdrakken, below the Little Scales Daycare (34.6, 60.6), or another Black Market broker. When the Golden Regal Scarab is listed, place the winning gold bid before the auction expires, then collect it from your mail. Inventory rotates and other players can outbid you; this mount has no fixed buyout or guaranteed listing day.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=211084), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/npc=189644)

## Grotto Netherwing Drake (mounts 431049, patch 10.2)

Complete the Wrathion and Vyranoth side story that opens during Guardians of the Dream. Pick up The Dragon Isles Await from Wrathion at the Central Encampment in the Emerald Dream, follow the Netherwing and storm-dragon errands, and finish Dragon Keeping to receive the Grotto Netherwing Drake. The July 2023 Trading Post reward was its original source; the quest chain provides a permanent way to obtain the mount.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/news/grotto-netherwing-drake-trading-post-mount-available-from-10-2-wrathion-quest-336291)

## Portable Party Platter (toys 207092, patch 10.2)

Buy the finished Portable Party Platter on the Auction House or have an engineer craft it. To learn the schematic yourself, reach Dragon Isles Engineering 50 and carry Light Parchment and Engineer's Ink. Stand near a mage who has a Chef's Splendid Rolling Pin equipped, knows a Dragon Isles dessert recipe, and gains the Well Fed buff by eating. The mage conjures a dessert and the nearby engineer receives the schematic. Learn it, craft the platter and use the finished item to add it to your Toy Box.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=207092/portable-party-platter)

## Royal Seafeather (mounts 254812, patch 10.2.6)

Original Plunderstorm acquisition: play the Plunderstorm event from the character-selection screen, collect Plunder through matches and its daily quest, then buy Royal Seafeather for 5,000 Plunder in the Plunderstore.

The first event also awarded it at Renown 20 with Keg Leg's Crew.

This recorded event has ended. The item is an event reward, with no ordinary world drop or permanent gold vendor; another opportunity depends on its inclusion in a returning Plunderstorm event.

Requires Renown 20 with Keg Leg's Crew.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/WorldEvents.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=233242)

## Stonevault Mechsuit (mounts 442358, patch 11.0)

Defeat High Speaker Eirich in The Stonevault on Mythic difficulty for a chance at the Malfunctioning Mechsuit. Use that item to begin the associated repair quest, take it to the repair contact in the Ringing Deeps, and complete the component errands through A Mech in Need. Finishing the repair chain awards the Stonevault Mechsuit. The source notes track the first unfinished quest once you have obtained the starter.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=84025), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=442358)

## Crowd Pummeler 2-30 (mounts 448186, patch 11.0)

Buy a completed Crowd Pummeler 2-30 on the Auction House, or build it with Khaz Algar Engineering. To learn the schematic, use Invent and the resulting Comprehensibly Organized Ideas until this recipe is offered. Craft it using 1 Pummel Permit, 1,000 Bountiful Bolts, 200 Assorted Whirligigs, 80 Blinker Fluid, 50 Cataclysmic Converters and 20 Pummel-Proof Plating. Paxton Bombshell at Opportunity Point in the Ringing Deeps sells the special parts; the Parts specialization also allows rare components to be recovered with Pilfer Through Parts. The finished mount can be traded, so you do not need Engineering to learn a purchased copy.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=221967/crowd-pummeler-2-30)

## Alunira (mounts 447213, patch 11.0)

Kill creatures on the Isle of Dorn until you collect 10 Crackling Shards. The shards bind to your Warband, so you can consolidate drops from your characters. Combine all ten into a Storm Vessel, fly to Alunira above Dhar Oztan (23.1, 58.5), and use your vessel to remove her Protective Stormshroud. Defeat her and loot the mount. Use your own vessel: merely tagging another player's summoned fight does not guarantee the mount. Alunira is an enemy whose shield consumes the vessel, not a merchant selling the mount.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/spell=447213/alunira)

## Dauntless Imperial Lynx (mounts 448979, patch 11.0)

Reach Renown 3 with the Hallowfall Arathi and go to the spreading-the-light area around Light's Blooming (65.4, 32.2). Farm Radiant Remnants from nearby enemies and use them to light Lesser and Major Keyflames. Complete the weekly quests and bonus objectives those flames unlock, then open their Lamplighter Supply Satchels for a chance at the Dauntless Imperial Lynx. Each flame's reward is limited by its weekly activity; a satchel is not a guaranteed mount.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=228741), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=223318)

## Siesbarg (mounts 451489, patch 11.0)

Defeat Tka'ktath near the City of Threads in Azj-Kahet (62.8, 66.6) and loot a Vial of Tka'ktath's Blood. Use it to start The Beginning of Something Beautiful, then take it to Shalba in the City of Threads (45.4, 13.5). Complete her gathering quests in order: 1,500 Nerubian Chitin, then 1,000 Nerubian Venom, then 500 Nerubian Blood. Kill nerubians for chitin and venom; ascended nerubians and skyrazors supply blood. Follower Ara-Kara runs are one farming option. Return each batch before gathering the next quest's items, watch Witnessing Brilliance, and finish Consolation Flies for Siesbarg.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=224150/siesbarg), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=83722)

Perform Thaumaturgy on eligible stacks of materials; Writhing Transmutagen is a rare bonus discovery from the process, rather than a directly craftable pet recipe. Repeat conversions if you want to obtain it yourself, or buy the learned pet in a cage from another player. No particular number of conversions guarantees the pet.

## Violet Sporbit (pets 4506, patch 11.0)

Finish Fungal Folly on the Isle of Dorn or Mycomancer Cavern in Hallowfall, then open the Heavy Trunks in the final treasure room for a chance at Violet Sporbit. It has also been reported from other delve completion chests, including ordinary low-tier runs; it does not require capturing a wild pet. Learn the dropped item or purchase a caged Violet Sporbit from another player.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wow-petguide.com/Pet/222590/Sporob%C3%AAte_violette?sel_lang=en), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=225337/violet-sporbit)

## Faithful Dog (pets 4596, patch 11.0)

First befriend Dog at the Heartland in Valley of the Four Winds by completing Lost and Lonely, then build the Herb Garden in your Warlords garrison so Dog moves in. Find a Loose Pebble on the streets of Legion Dalaran and show it to Dog in your garrison so he moves to Dalaran. On the Isle of Dorn, inspect the Half-Buried Dog Bowl at (31.4, 51.3) near Dalaran's crash site, then interact with Dog there and pet his head to learn Faithful Dog. The source notes follow your first unfinished step, so an established friendship does not need to be repeated.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/battle-pet/4596), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=83093), [source 3](https://www.wowhead.com/quest=83094)

## Incognitro, the Indecipherable Felcycle (mounts 428013, patch 11.0.5)

Unlock the Detective title through Azeroth's Greatest Detective and obtain the Torch of Pyrreth from Secrets of Azeroth. Equip Detective and begin Ratts' Race with the Dalaran Survivor in Dornogal (54.9, 28.9). Read the notes in Azj-Kahet (69.3, 93.3), above the City of Threads (31.5, 20.8), and underwater in Hallowfall (50.7, 86.6). Confront Ratts in the hidden upper tunnel inside Pillar-nest Vosh, Azj-Kahet (entrance 55.1, 19.0; climb at 56.4, 17.5), and take the Peculiar Gem. Use the torch in the rotten tree trunk in northern Un'Goro (44.5, 8.0) for the Inert Peculiar Key. Combine them, carry the resulting key to the Karazhan Catacombs gate in Deadwind Pass (46.3, 69.1), and use the torch to enter Ratts' Revenge. Solve the following nine basins in order. You do not need the later flight-unlock puzzles to obtain the mount.

1. With your torch active, wait for The Light of Their Love at the Humble Monument in Northern Barrens (55.0, 40.2), Young Olgra in Draenor Nagrand (74.2, 37.5 or 49.2, 48.0 depending on your story phase), and Decimator Olgra in Maldraxxus (27.3, 61.3). Obtain all three stacks.

2. At the Ny'alotha Obelisk above the Seat of Knowledge in the BFA phase of Vale of Eternal Blossoms (83.7, 27.6), summon Perky Pug wearing a Dogg-Saron or Yipp-Saron costume, use Twitching Eyeball or All-Seeing Eyes for Gift of N'Zoth, and /pray. Wait nearby for the Key of Shadows. Another player can fulfill these requirements for everyone standing within ten yards.

3. Back in the catacombs, open the chamber beside the Red Button with the key. Fish an Astral Key from the bowl, open the Astral Chest and equip Starry-Eyed Goggles. Use the consoles and loot their Pieces of Hate. Catacombs coordinates and codes: (48.9, 80.3) 88224646; (42.9, 70.6) 17112317; (49.5, 65.1) try 1533, 3457, 8265 or 10638; (67.4, 84.3) 19019; (56.3, 62.6) 5661; (64.9, 48.3) 52233; (70.3, 55.4) 51567; (66.3, 15.2) 115. For the ninth piece, carry at least five distinct lucky items, for example Lucky Rat's Tooth, Lucky Shirt, Lucky Horseshoe, Lucky Duck and Lucky Dragon's Claw, then enter 777 at a slot-machine console in the felcycle room. Keep all nine pieces.

4. Buy a Scroll of Fel Binding from Vashti the Wandering Merchant in Azsuna. Use it outside Uther's Tomb in Western Plaguelands (52.1, 85.1), return after dying, and read all four writings while the summoned doomguard is alive.

5. On Timeless Isle, ask Zarhym in the Cavern of Lost Spirits to enter the spirit realm. Reach Jeremy Feasel before the spirit timer expires. Defeat him with three level-25 secret pets, such as Baa'l, Wicker Pup and Sun Darter Hatchling; a grouped player's successful battle can also qualify you. Accept Jeremy's quest and obtain the Relic of Crystal Connections.

6. Use your torch at the five acquisition altars in northeastern Northern Stranglethorn. One player must satisfy each altar's complete combination; friends can help with different altars. Blood (77.1, 46.3): a mount and pet with Blood in their names, plus Throbbing Blood Orb. Corruption (77.5, 43.9): Cloak of Overwhelming Corruption appearance, a Corrupted mount and pet, Ring of Broken Promises or Accursed Tome of the Sargerei, and /cower. Void (78.1, 46.3): Cloak of the Black Void appearance, Lesser Voidcaller or another qualifying void pet, Shadowy Disguise or Void Totem, and /smirk. Sin (78.3, 44.0): a sinstone back appearance, Sinheart, and Bondable Sinstone. Lust (77.1, 44.9): remove your clothes, summon Sister of Temptation, use Moroes' Famous Polish or Steamy Romance Novel Kit, and /flirt. With goggles equipped, loot Ancient Shaman Blood from the chest by the altars (78.2, 47.7).

7. Summon Fledgling Warden Owl on Azsuna's Isle of the Watchers. Find the active owl statues and stand with your owl in each of the red, green, blue and purple auras until each triggers the magnifying-glass effect. Search the island around (40.5, 74.0), (43.5, 86.0), (47.5, 84.7) and (50.4, 91.7); the active statues rotate. Keep the empowered owl summoned and clear Vault of the Wardens. Take Elune's Light from Cordana's arena, return to the newly opened room before the first boss and collect the Sentry Statue. Place it in the Vault of Mirrors. Lower every statue in its five-by-five toggle puzzle to loot Warden's Mirror.

8. Insert Ancient Shaman Blood and Warden's Mirror into the Enigma Machine at catacombs (59.9, 42.6). Begin, then submit to release Rats. Count and kill every Rat, excluding creatures named Catacombs Rat; kill those as well so they cannot stand on plates. Complete three locks. For each lock multiply the Rat count by the lock number; subtract 20 if the result exceeds 20. Count that result along the seven plates repeatedly: 1-7 means one statue on plate 1-7, 8-14 means two statues on plate 1-7, and 15-20 means three statues on plate 1-6. Submit the arrangement, clear the plate and repeat for the next lock. Plate coordinates, in order: (71.6, 20.1), (68.5, 34.2), (73.6, 43.0), (68.8, 50.9), (73.6, 65.3), (60.2, 71.6), (47.8, 78.9). Reach plate 3 by targeting its Humming Crystal and using the relic. A wrong submission requires clearing the Rats and restarting.

9. Return to the hidden upper tunnel in Pillar-nest Vosh. Wear the goggles to reveal the platform and use the relic on its Humming Crystal. Submit 84847078 at the console, then open the Encrypted Chest to learn Incognitro.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Secrets.lua), [source 2](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/incognitro-the-indecipherable-felcycle)

## Thrayir, Eyes of the Siren (mounts 471562, patch 11.0.7)

Finish Siren Isle's story, then enter Seafury Tempest through Suzie Boltwrench (69.0, 49.2). Collect five runekeys during the storm. Cyclonic drops from Zek'ul at (33.0, 73.6), or fishing nearby. Whirling drops from Ksvir in the Forgotten Vault. Make Turbulent from three fragments at (38.2, 51.9), (67.2, 78.8) and inside the cave starting at (50.2, 42.4). Make Torrential from seven fragments dropped by storm enemies, and Thunderous from five fragments in Rune Storm Caches revealed with Runecaster's Eye. Enter the vault via the cave at (44.8, 22.4) and Singing Tablet at (50.2, 15.5). Use the keys on the stones surrounding Thrayir in the vault (72.7, 61.4), then accept his quest to learn the mount.

Evidence: [source 1](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/thrayir)

## Personalized Goblin S.C.R.A.P.per (mounts 466020, patch 11.1)

Open Sifted Pile of Scrap in Undermine (25.8, 38.1) for a chance at Personalized Goblin S.C.R.A.P.per. Obtain the container with 333 Empty Kaja'Cola Can.

Frequently around the zone a pile of scrap will be marked on the map, ready for excavation. Once started, there is a two minute timer to dig up as much as possible, clearing obstacles and dodging projectiles. Renown unlocks new drops and efficiency upgrades, and a rare mob can sometimes spawn at max excavation progress.

## Salvaged Goblin Gazillionaire's Flying Machine (mounts 466026, patch 11.1)

Defeat Gallagio Garbage in Undermine and loot Salvaged Goblin Gazillionaire's Flying Machine. This is a chance drop.

Frequently around the zone a pile of scrap will be marked on the map, ready for excavation. Once started, there is a two minute timer to dig up as much as possible, clearing obstacles and dodging projectiles. Renown unlocks new drops and efficiency upgrades, and a rare mob can sometimes spawn at max excavation progress.

Can spawn when players fill the 'Trash Shoveled' progress bar to 500 during a S.C.R.A.P. Job event in Undermine. Note: All loot can drop even when the daily flag is saved.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Zones.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=229953)

## Darkfuse Spy-Eye (mounts 466027, patch 11.1)

Defeat Darkfuse Precipitant in Undermine (41.2, 91.4) and loot Darkfuse Spy-Eye. This is a chance drop.

## The Big G (mounts 1217760, patch 11.1)

Enter Liberation of Undermine through the Incontinental Hotel in Undermine (42.0, 50.2) on Mythic difficulty. Defeat the final boss, Chrome King Gallywix, and loot The Big G. Normal, Heroic and Raid Finder do not award this mount. You have one eligible Mythic boss loot opportunity per character each weekly reset, and the mount is shared raid loot rather than a personal reward for every participant.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235626)

## Prototype A.S.M.R. (mounts 1221155, patch 11.1)

Defeat Chrome King Gallywix, the final boss of Liberation of Undermine, and check the boss loot for Prototype A.S.M.R. It can drop on Raid Finder, Normal, Heroic or Mythic difficulty. The raid entrance is in the Incontinental Hotel in Undermine (42.0, 50.2). This is a rare drop with an eligible loot opportunity per difficulty each weekly reset; the different mount, The Big G, is Mythic only.

## Undermine Supply Crate (toys 237382, patch 11.1)

Buy Undermine Supply Crate from Angelo Rustbin in Undermine (25.8, 38.1) for 999 Empty Kaja'Cola Can.

Frequently around the zone a pile of scrap will be marked on the map, ready for excavation. Once started, there is a two minute timer to dig up as much as possible, clearing obstacles and dodging projectiles. Renown unlocks new drops and efficiency upgrades, and a rare mob can sometimes spawn at max excavation progress.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/Zones.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=237382)

## Voidfire Deathcycle (mounts 428068, patch 11.1.5)

Enter the revisited Horrific Vision of Stormwind with at least one Faceless Mask active. Defeat Haymar the Devout in the Dwarven District and send his bike frame to Dornogal. Rebuild it at Dornogal (33.6, 68.0). Collect the manifold near Stormwind's Trade District auction house, handlebars from Valeera's chest, crystal from Umbric's chest, and wheel materials from Wyrmbane's chest. In the Orgrimmar rotation, collect the engine block in the Drag, boarhide from Rexxar's chest and wheel materials from Geya'rah's chest. Each part needs an active mask. Combine them with the crafting reagents shown by the frame, then install the repaired engine, exhaust, handlebars, battery, seat and two wheels. Stormwind and Orgrimmar alternate, so plan for both rotations.

Evidence: [source 1](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/voidfire-deathcycle)

## Nesting Swarmite (mounts 447189, patch 11.1.5)

Defeat Nesting Swarmite in Horrific Vision of Orgrimmar (40.3, 79.4) and loot Nesting Swarmite. This is a chance drop.

Has a chance to spawn from piles of Trash in either vision.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/ExpansionFeatures.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=223265)

## Void-Scarred Gryphon (mounts 1218229, patch 11.1.5)

Enter the revisited Horrific Vision of Stormwind with at least two Faceless Masks active and clear the Trade District. Read the two ripped notes near (68.6, 73.3) and (65.7, 71.6). Match the requested ingredient and preparation: raw fish = Fresh Fillet; cooked fish = Skewered Fillet; raw fungus = Chopped Mycobloom; cooked fungus = Roasted Mycobloom; raw spiced meat = Spiced Meat Stock; cooked spiced meat = Simple Stew; raw steak = Portioned Steak; cooked steak = Unseasoned Field Steak. Place the matching food in the Claw Marked Bowl (68.1, 73.4), rattle it, then defeat the arriving Void-Scarred Gryphon and loot its reins.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235700/reins-of-the-void-scarred-gryphon)

## Void-Forged Stallion (mounts 1218305, patch 11.1.5)

Defeat Void-Forged Stallion in Horrific Vision of Stormwind (50.9, 84.0) and loot Void-Forged Stallion. This is a chance drop.

Interact with Cursed Horseshoes around Stormwind's districts to gain a stacking buff, visit the blacksmithing area in the Dwarven District to summon the rare when you have 4 stacks. Requires an active Mask

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/ExpansionFeatures.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235705)

## Void-Scarred Pack Mother (mounts 1218306, patch 11.1.5)

Defeat Void-Scarred Wolf in Horrific Vision of Orgrimmar (39.2, 49.5) and loot Void-Scarred Pack Mother. This is a chance drop.

Interact with Wolf Rider gear in the Valleys of Honor and Wisdom to gain a stacking buff, then interact with a wolf rug at the Drag's leatherworking shop to spawn the rare. Wolf rider packs are only visible with an active Mask.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/ExpansionFeatures.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235706)

## Void-Scarred Windrider (mounts 1218307, patch 11.1.5)

Defeat Void-Scarred Wyvern Matriarch in Horrific Vision of Orgrimmar (48.9, 51.6) and loot Void-Scarred Windrider. This is a chance drop.

Clear the Valley of Wisdom to access the elevator up to the Skyway. Defeat waves of Void-Scarred Wyverns until the matriarch spawns. The path up to the Skyway will remain blocked without at least 3 masks active.

Evidence: [source 1](https://github.com/ATTWoWAddon/AllTheThings/blob/f8113a527789bf361e070d7cde94d1f57a22b2b8/db/Standard/Categories/ExpansionFeatures.lua), [source 2](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235707)

## Void-Crystal Panther (mounts 1218317, patch 11.1.5)

Use a character with Khaz Algar Jewelcrafting 30. Enter the revisited Horrific Vision of Orgrimmar with at least one Faceless Mask and defeat Big Keech in the Valley of Honor (72.4, 34.9) for the design and Void-Bound Orb of Mystery. With the orb acquired and a mask active, collect 20 Shadow Infused Onyx from corrupted-area chests, Unusual Gems from Valeera's chest after clearing Old Town in Stormwind, and 4 Black Blood Infused Bars from the vision blacksmithing areas. Learn the design and craft the panther at a jeweler's bench. The finished mount binds to your Warband; it is not an ordinary Auction House panther.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=235712), [source 2](https://warcraft.wiki.gg/wiki/Design%3A_Void-Crystal_Panther)

## Xy Trustee's Gearglider (mounts 353264, patch 11.2)

Reach Renown 8 with the Manaforge Vandals. Obtain the Inscription items Deal: Cartel Ba, Deal: Cartel Zo and Deal: Cartel Om, by crafting them or buying them on the Auction House. Use one deal per account each week and equip its cartel title. While aligned, collect that cartel's hidden cypher inside Manaforge Omega: Ba is after Plexus Sentinel near the phase-warp ledge; Zo is on the mana-vent pipes before Forgeweaver Araz; Om is on a rock in the area opened after Fractillus. Leave Araz alive while collecting Ba and Zo. After all three cyphers, return to Zo'turu in K'aresh (42.0, 22.1) and finish Someone Like Me for the mount and Cartel Transmorpher. Allow three weekly cartel selections.

Evidence: [source 1](https://warcraft-secrets.com/guides/xy-trustees-gearglider)

## Vandal's Gearglider (mounts 353265, patch 11.2)

Complete Welcome to the Team from Zo'turu in K'aresh (42.0, 22.1) to receive Vandal's Gearglider.

My friend, we have made much progress together in bringing down the Shadowguard. For your sacrifices and efforts, my colleagues and I consider you one of us. Please, take this modified Gearglider as a show of our gratitiude - may it carry you safely to your next mission.

Requires Renown 8 with Manaforge Vandals.

Earn Phase-Lost-and-Found: 10 Points Account-wide progress Collect all of the Phase-Lost item appearances found from Phase Diving orbs. - Phase-Lost Longsword - Phase-Lost Hammer - Phase-Lost Carver - Phase-Lost Zweihander - Phase-Lost Maul - Phase-Lost Chopper - Phase-Lost Bardiche - Phase-Lost Spire - Phase-Lost Blunderbuss - Phase-Lost Baselard - Phase-Lost Katar - Phase-Lost Sigil - Phase-Lost Bulwark - Phase-Lost Rod - Phase-Lost Scimitar - Phase-Lost Cudgel - Phase-Lost Hatchet - Phase-Lost Greatsword - Phase-Lost Great Mace - Phase-Lost Battleaxe - Phase-Lost Spear - Phase-Lost Shillelagh - Phase-Lost Longbow - Phase-Lost Dirk - Phase-Lost Claw - Phase-Lost Beacon - Phase-Lost Pavise - Phase-Lost Wand

## Stormforged Grapple Launcher (toys 138111, patch 11.2.5)

Complete Stormforged Grapple Launcher from Stormforged Grapple Launcher in Stormheim (33.8, 45.7) to receive Stormforged Grapple Launcher.

## Bonecleaver's Skullboar (mounts 332482, patch 11.2.7)

This mount has no confirmed obtainable version. The advertised Shadowlands Timewalking purchase did not appear on Collector Ta'steld, and Sabriel the Bonecleaver's beta drop was never released. Do not farm Sabriel or spend Timewarped Badges expecting this mount; his live mount reward is Armored Bonehoof Tauralus. Keep this entry as an unreleased collectible until an actual acquisition source is confirmed.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=182083/bonecleavers-skullboar), [source 2](https://us.forums.blizzard.com/en/wow/t/bonecleavers-skullboar/2076393)

## Skypaw Glimmerfur (mounts 1263369, patch 11.2.7)

During Shadowlands Timewalking, visit Collector Ta'steld in the Ring of Fates in Oribos and buy Skypaw Glimmerfur for 5,000 Timewarped Badges. Earn the badges from Timewalking dungeons, raids and their event quests. The vendor follows the active Timewalking schedule, so return during the Shadowlands event if the item is absent.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/guide/collections/the-war-within/mounts-sources-patch-11-2-7)

## Brawlin' Bruno (mounts 1264621, patch 11.2.7)

Join the Brawler's Guild in your faction's arena: Bizmo's Brawlpub beneath the Deeprun Tram in Stormwind, or Brawl'gar Arena in Orgrimmar. Win the ranked solo encounters until rank 6, then buy Brawlin' Bruno from the arena quartermaster for 4,000 gold. Each rank requires defeating its encounter sequence; random exhibition fights do not substitute for rank progression. The purchase is available during an active Brawler's Guild season.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=259227/brawlin-bruno)

## Ballistic Bronco (mounts 1264643, patch 11.2.7)

Progress through the ranked Brawler's Guild encounters in Bizmo's Brawlpub beneath Stormwind's Deeprun Tram or Brawl'gar Arena in Orgrimmar. Reach rank 8, then buy Ballistic Bronco from the arena quartermaster for 10,000 gold. Work through the rank bosses in order; exhibition fights do not advance the required rank. This route requires an active Brawler's Guild season.

Evidence: [source 1](https://www.wowhead.com/item=259238/ballistic-bronco)
